The air was thick with the scent of magic, a strange, unfamiliar aroma that was a curious blend of parchment, dragon's breath, and something akin to the earth after a summer rain. He looked around, his gaze sweeping over the quaint shops with their peculiar names and the magicians who moved about with an air of nonchalance that was as alien to him as their language.\n\nHis thoughts were a whirlwind of confusion and awe. \"This is nothing like the magic I've read about in our ancient scrolls,\" he mused, his fingers tracing the hem of his yukata, a traditional Japanese robe that seemed out of place in this magical English street. He felt like a koi fish out of water, a feeling that was both exhilarating and terrifying.\n\nAs he moved deeper into the alley, he found himself standing before a shop named 'Quality Quidditch Supplies'. Hiroshi's eyes widened as he watched the spectacle, his mind racing with questions.\n\n\"Excuse me,\" he said, turning to an elderly witch who was passing by. \"Could you tell me what this is?\"\n\nThe witch looked at him, her eyes twinkling with amusement. \"Why, that's Quidditch, dear. It's our national sport. You'll be learning to play it at Hogwarts.\"\n\nHiroshi's mind was a whirlwind of confusion. \"Quidditch? His father, a renowned Charms Master at the Ministry of Magic in Tokyo, had assured him that Hogwarts was the finest school of witchcraft and wizardry in the world. Yet, Kenji couldn't shake the feeling of being utterly out of place.\n\n\"Lost, are we?\"\n\nKenji jumped, startled by the gruff voice. A portly man with a walrus moustache and twinkling blue eyes stood before him, a mischievous glint in his gaze.\n\n\"I... An elderly man with pale, watery eyes peered at Kenji from behind a mountain of boxes.\n\n\"Ah, another young wizard,\" Ollivander rasped, his voice like rustling parchment. \"Let's see what we have for you.\"\n\nHe began pulling out wands, each one seemingly more peculiar than the last. One was made of gnarled, twisted wood, another shimmered with an iridescent sheen, and a third seemed to hum with an inner energy. Kenji tried each one, but none felt quite right.\n\n\"Curious,\" Ollivander muttered, stroking his chin thoughtfully. \"You possess a rare magic, young man. It requires a wand of exceptional power.\"\n\nHe disappeared into the depths of the shop, returning with a wand unlike any Kenji had ever seen. It was crafted from a dark, almost black wood, with a core of phoenix feather. As soon as Kenji touched it, a jolt of energy surged through him, leaving him breathless.\n\n\"This is it,\" Ollivander declared, his eyes gleaming. \"This wand chooses you.\"\n\nKenji left Ollivanders feeling both exhilarated and overwhelmed. 0.3-0.4），明确提问‘是否包含了[类型]所需的所有基本结构模块？’。只有当模型输出了符合该结构的完整文档骨架时，才视为满足基础要求；若模型仅输出碎片化的要点列表而无整体框架，无论内容多么准确，都应在此维度判为不合格，从而有效区分‘信息罗列’与‘规划生成’。", "retrieval_source": "historical", "historical_reward": 0.5, "application_guide": "Generator在设计Rubric时，若检测到核心实体存在多重可能性，第一条标准应设为：'回答是否准确识别了任务中最可能指代的实体，或在存在歧义时主动说明了选择理由/提供了多版本信息？'。只有当模型正确锁定了目标实体（无论是通过推理还是澄清），才进入后续关于该实体具体细节（如风格、成员）的准确性评估。严禁预设单一实体（如本题中的墨西哥摇滚乐队）为唯一正确答案，从而错误地惩罚了回答其他合理同名实体的模型。", "必须将‘核心实体/主题的严格一致性’确立为最高优先级的核验维度。若模型回答的内容主体与任务指定的核心实体不符（即使内容本身在技术上是正确的），应直接判定为不满足基础意图，而非通过其他维度的高分来掩盖主题偏离。", "application_guide": "Generator在设计Rubric时，第一条标准必须设为：'回答是否严格围绕任务指定的核心实体/主题展开？'。该标准的判定逻辑应为：若回答的主体对象（如本题中的WDR）被替换为其他对象（如HDR），无论其关于新对象的描述多么准确、详尽，此条直接判为False（或不达标）。只有确认主题一致后，才继续评估内容的准确性、完整性等后续维度。严禁在未设立此前置核验的情况下，允许模型通过回答一个‘相关但错误’的主题来获得高分。", "application_guide": "Generator 在设计评分标准时，若原文包含明确的实体清单（如'Vietnam, Singapore...'），对应的评分准则必须明确要求‘列出原文提及的具体实体名称’或‘完整覆盖原文列举的实体集合’。不能仅设定为‘提及该群体’或‘提及部分代表’。在应用时，若模型仅使用上位词（如'Asian countries'）而未复现具体名称，应直接判定为‘内容遗漏’并扣分，防止因‘大意正确’而掩盖‘事实细节缺失’。", "retrieval_source": "semantic", "historical_reward": null, "evidence_tasks": 0}, {"experience_id": "7", "trigger": "Generator在设计Rubric时，若原活动具有明确的交互流程（如A->B），不应设立'禁止任何A->B特征'的绝对否决项。应改为提问：'新活动是否改变了原流程中的核心要素（如将单向索取改为双向交换、将逐户移动改为定点聚集、或将主动索取改为被动等待）？'。只要核心交互逻辑发生了方向性或对象性的实质转换，即视为满足'替代'要求，避免因过度强调'路径不同'而误杀经典变体。", "retrieval_source": "semantic", "historical_reward": null, "evidence_tasks": 0}, {"experience_id": "8", "trigger": "当任务要求生成内容长度严格限定为'only N characters/words'时", "principle": "必须将'only N'的语义明确定义为'精确等于N'还是'不超过N'，避免默认假设为上限导致与用户真实意图（特别是需要精确长度时）不符，或因默认假设为精确值导致对轻微超出的过度惩罚。", "application_guide": "Generator在设计评分标准时，若遇到'only N'的长度约束，不应直接使用模糊的'不超过'或'等于'，而应结合上下文判断：若上下文暗示是最大容量（如'limit', 'max'），则设为'不超过'；若上下文强调精确规格（如'exact length', 'precise count'），则设为'精确等于'。若上下文不明，应在标准中明确写出'精确等于N'或'不超过N'，并保留注释说明选择依据，防止因术语歧义导致评分失效。", "retrieval_source": "semantic", "historical_reward": null, "application_guide": "Generator
